PRODUCT: ADAudit Plus
=============================================================================

- Source: https://www.manageengine.com/products/active-directory-audit/
  Title: Active Directory Auditing and Compliance Solution
  Description: ADAudit Plus provides comprehensive Active Directory monitoring, change auditing, and user behavior analytics to enhance IT compliance and security. IT administrators can streamline auditing processes, demonstrate compliance effortlessly, and detect potential threats with minimal effort. This page details the capabilities of ADAudit Plus, specifically designed for organizations seeking robust auditing solutions.

- Source: https://www.manageengine.com/products/active-directory-audit/account-lockout-examiner.html
  Title: Real-Time Account Lockout Analysis Tool
  Description: Enables IT administrators to analyze and troubleshoot user account lockouts in Windows Active Directory in real time. It provides notifications via email and SMS alerts, helping to minimize downtime for employees. The solution includes pre-configured reports to streamline the troubleshooting process.

- Source: https://www.manageengine.com/products/active-directory-audit/account-logon-events/event-id-4770.html
  Title: Understanding Event ID 4770 in Kerberos
  Description: Event ID 4770 details the renewal of a Kerberos service ticket, crucial for IT administrators monitoring account logon events. This page provides insights into the significance of this event and its implications for Active Directory auditing. IT professionals can leverage this information to enhance security and compliance within their organizations.

- Source: https://www.manageengine.com/products/active-directory-audit/account-logon-events/event-id-4774.html
  Title: Understanding Event ID 4774 in Auditing
  Description: Event ID 4774 highlights the process of mapping an account for logon, providing critical information for tracking user access and permissions. This event is essential for identifying potential security risks associated with account logon activities. IT administrators can utilize this data to refine their auditing processes and ensure compliance with security policies.

- Source: https://www.manageengine.com/products/active-directory-audit/account-logon-events/event-id-4777.html
